4	// liquidDirty → 同快进但 3 tick 节流(20Hz)。
5	// 替换原 BFS 引擎(备份见 docs/backup/LightingEngine.legacy.bak.ts)。
6	import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
7	import { TileStore } from '../world/TileStore';
8	import { AREA_PADDING, GLOBAL_BRIGHTNESS, NON_VISIBLE_PADDING } from './lightTables';
9	import { FlickerClock } from './SkyColor';
10	import { LightMap } from './LightMap';
11	import { PerFrameLight, ScannerWorld, TileLightScanner } from './TileLightScanner';
12	
13	/** 引擎依赖的最小世界结构(World 结构兼容;单测可注入 fake) */
14	export interface LightingWorld {
15	  store: TileStore;
16	  groundLevel: number;  // 原版 worldSurface
17	  lavaLine: number;     // 原版 UnderworldLayer
18	  clock: { timeOfDay: number; dayCount: number };
19	}
20	
21	const enum State { MinimapUpdate = 0, ExportMetrics = 1, Scan = 2, Blur = 3 }
22	
23	export class LightingEngine {
24	  world: LightingWorld;
25	  // ---- 公共面(与 Game.ts/Renderer.ts 契约,勿改) ----
26	  rx = 0; ry = 0; rw = 0; rh = 0;
27	  r: Uint8Array = new Uint8Array(0);
28	  g: Uint8Array = new Uint8Array(0);
29	  b: Uint8Array = new Uint8Array(0);
30	  dirty = true;
31	  liquidDirty = false;
32	  heldLight: { tx: number; ty: number; r: number; g: number; b: number } | null = null;
33	  /** 动态点光 API(原版 Lighting.AddLight;0-1 RGB)——每周期消费后清空 */
34	  addLight(x: number, y: number, r: number, g: number, b: number): void {
35	    this.perFrame.push({ x, y, r, g, b });
36	    this.dirty = true;
37	  }
38	
39	  /** 本帧已注册的动态点光数（F5 调试报告只读 perFrame 长度） */
40	  get perFrameCount(): number { return this.perFrame.length; }
41	
42	  // ---- 内部 ----
43	  private state: State = State.MinimapUpdate;
44	  private work = new LightMap();
45	  private active = new LightMap();
46	  private flicker = new FlickerClock();
47	
48	  /** 全局闪烁时钟(mouseTextColor/cursorScale 等),资源条 HUD 等渲染层读取 */
49	  get clock(): FlickerClock { return this.flicker; }
50	  private scanner: TileLightScanner;
51	  private perFrame: PerFrameLight[] = [];
52	  private liqTick = 0;
53	
54	  constructor(world: LightingWorld) {
55	    this.world = world;
56	    this.scanner = new TileLightScanner(this.flicker);
57	    world.store.onTileChanged(() => { this.dirty = true; });
58	    world.store.onLiquidChanged(() => { this.liquidDirty = true; });
59	  }
60	
61	  /**
62	   * 区域 = 相机视口 ± AREA_PADDING(原版 GetAreaToLight 边界 1..2 + ProcessScan 膨胀 28 合并)。
63	   * camCX/camCY 为像素;viewW/viewH 为 tile 数(Game 现有调用约定)。
64	   */
65	  ensureRegion(camCX: number, camCY: number, viewW: number, viewH: number): void {
66	    const st = this.world.store;
67	    const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(camCX / TILE) - Math.ceil(viewW / 2) - 1 - AREA_PADDING);
68	    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(camCY / TILE) - Math.ceil(viewH / 2) - 1 - AREA_PADDING);
69	    const x1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor(camCX / TILE) + Math.ceil(viewW / 2) + 2 + AREA_PADDING);
70	    const y1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, Math.floor(camCY / TILE) + Math.ceil(viewH / 2) + 2 + AREA_PADDING);
71	    const rw = x1 - x0 + 1, rh = y1 - y0 + 1;
72	    if (rw !== this.rw || rh !== this.rh || x0 !== this.rx || y0 !== this.ry) {
73	      // 原点只在重算时原子移动(防光影相对世界平移)
74	      this.rx = x0; this.ry = y0;
75	      if (rw !== this.rw || rh !== this.rh) {
76	        this.rw = rw; this.rh = rh;
77	        this.r = new Uint8Array(rw * rh);
78	        this.g = new Uint8Array(rw * rh);
79	        this.b = new Uint8Array(rw * rh);
80	      }
81	      this.dirty = true;
82	    }
83	  }
84	
85	  /** 每帧调用。dayFactor 参数弃用(天空色改由 clock 内部推导;旧调用方签名兼容)。 */
86	  compute(_dayFactor: number): void {
87	    if (this.rw === 0) return;
88	    this.flicker.tick();
89	
90	    if (this.dirty) {
91	      this.runFullCycle();
92	      return;
93	    }
94	    if (this.liquidDirty) {
95	      this.liqTick++;
96	      if (this.liqTick % 3 !== 0) return; // 20Hz 节流
97	      this.runFullCycle();
98	      return;
99	    }
100	    // 空闲:每 tick 一态(完整周期 4 tick,原版 4 帧节奏)
101	    this.step();
102	  }
103	
104	  /** 快进:同 tick 完成 Scan→Blur→Present(状态回 0) */
105	  private runFullCycle(): void {
106	    this.dirty = false;
107	    this.liquidDirty = false;
108	    this.liqTick = 0;
109	    this.processScan();
110	    this.processBlurAndPresent();
111	    this.state = State.MinimapUpdate;
112	  }
113	
114	  private step(): void {
115	    switch (this.state) {
116	      case State.MinimapUpdate: // 空槽(小地图导出未移植;保留维持 4 tick 周期)
117	      case State.ExportMetrics: // 空槽(SceneMetrics 在 VanillaSpawner 独立实现)
118	        break;
119	      case State.Scan:
120	        this.processScan();
121	        break;
122	      case State.Blur:
123	        this.processBlurAndPresent();
124	        break;
125	    }
126	    this.state = (this.state + 1) % 4;
127	  }
128	
129	  private processScan(): void {
130	    const w = this.world as ScannerWorld;
131	    this.work.setSize(this.rw, this.rh);
132	    this.work.nonVisiblePadding = Math.min(NON_VISIBLE_PADDING, (Math.min(this.rw, this.rh) - 1) >> 1);
133	    // 手持光源:RGB 来自 TorchID.TorchColor(原版 ItemCheck_EmitHeldItemLight 按火把种类取色)
134	    const perFrame = this.perFrame;
135	    if (this.heldLight) perFrame.push({ x: this.heldLight.tx, y: this.heldLight.ty, r: this.heldLight.r, g: this.heldLight.g, b: this.heldLight.b });
136	    this.scanner.exportTo(w, this.work, this.rx, this.ry, perFrame);
137	    perFrame.length = 0;
138	  }
139	
140	  private processBlurAndPresent(): void {
141	    this.work.blur();
142	    // Present(LightingEngine.cs:131-135):交换双缓冲 + 导出 ×1.2 到公共面
143	    const tmp = this.active;
144	    this.active = this.work;
145	    this.work = tmp;
146	    const outR = this.r, outG = this.g, outB = this.b;
147	    const ar = this.active.r, ag = this.active.g, ab = this.active.b;
148	    const scale = GLOBAL_BRIGHTNESS * 255;
149	    for (let i = 0; i < outR.length; i++) {
150	      outR[i] = Math.min(255, Math.round(ar[i] * scale));
151	      outG[i] = Math.min(255, Math.round(ag[i] * scale));
152	      outB[i] = Math.min(255, Math.round(ab[i] * scale));
153	    }
154	  }
155	
156	  /** 取某 tile 光照(区域外返回 0;原版 GetColor 区域外语义) */
157	  lightAt(tx: number, ty: number, _dayFactor = 0): [number, number, number] {
158	    if (tx < this.rx || ty < this.ry || tx >= this.rx + this.rw || ty >= this.ry + this.rh) return [0, 0, 0];
159	    const i = (ty - this.ry) * this.rw + (tx - this.rx);
160	    return [this.r[i], this.g[i], this.b[i]];
161	  }
162	}

1	// 光传播核心:2 pass × 4 向单调线扫描"Blur"(LightMap.cs:86-254 一比一)。
2	// 非洪泛:每条扫描线携带一路 running 光值 + 三通道死标记,逐格
3	//   ① cell>zero → zero=cell(源格,复活)
4	//   ② 否则 !dead → zero<0.0185 死 / cell=zero
5	//   ③ 按当前格 mask 衰减 zero
6	// 双缓冲由引擎层持有(本类单图);行主序布局(与导出面一致)。
7	import { FastRandom } from './FastRandom';
8	import { DEAD_THRESHOLD, DECAY_AIR, DECAY_CRACKED, DECAY_HONEY, DECAY_SOLID, DECAY_WATER } from './lightTables';
9	
10	/** 遮挡类型(LightMaskMode.cs;注意 Water=2 Honey=3 CrackedBricks=4) */
11	export const enum Mask { None = 0, Solid = 1, Water = 2, Honey = 3, CrackedBricks = 4 }
12	
13	export class LightMap {
14	  w = 0; h = 0;
15	  /** 三通道 SoA(浮点保真:Uint8 会把 0.0185 死阈值量化成可见条带) */
16	  r = new Float32Array(0);
17	  g = new Float32Array(0);
18	  b = new Float32Array(0);
19	  mask = new Uint8Array(0);
20	  /** 不可见边距(LightMap.cs:30 引擎注入;Blur 扫描跳过外圈该厚度) */
21	  nonVisiblePadding = 0;
22	  /** 水 flicker 随机源(每格 WithModifier(i)) */
23	  private random = new FastRandom((Math.random() * 1e9) | 0);
24	  /** 周期索引(引擎每 Present 递增;掺进水随机种子 → 每 4 tick 换一批微光) */
25	  cycleIndex = 0;
26	
27	  setSize(w: number, h: number): void {
28	    if (this.w === w && this.h === h && this.r.length >= w * h) return;
29	    this.w = w; this.h = h;
30	    const n = w * h;
31	    this.r = new Float32Array(n);
32	    this.g = new Float32Array(n);
33	    this.b = new Float32Array(n);
34	    this.mask = new Uint8Array(n);
35	  }
36	
37	  clear(): void {
38	    this.r.fill(0); this.g.fill(0); this.b.fill(0);
39	  }
40	
41	  /** Blur()(L86-91):两遍 BlurPass + NextSeed */
42	  blur(): void {
43	    this.blurPass();
44	    this.blurPass();
45	    this.random = this.random.withModifier(++this.cycleIndex);
46	  }
47	
48	  /** BlurPass()(L93-115):列(上→下 / 下→上)后行(左→右 / 右→左) */
49	  private blurPass(): void {
50	    const { w, h, mask } = this;
51	    const pad = Math.min(this.nonVisiblePadding, Math.max(0, Math.min(w, h) >> 1));
52	    // 列扫描(行主序:跨行跳 rw)
53	    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
54	      this.blurLine(x, 0, x, h - 1 - pad, 1);
55	      this.blurLine(x, h - 1, x, pad, -1);
56	    }
57	    // 行扫描(行主序:连续内存)
58	    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
59	      this.blurLine(0, y, w - 1 - pad, y, 1);
60	      this.blurLine(w - 1, y, pad, y, -1);
61	    }
62	    void mask;
63	  }
64	
65	  /**
66	   * BlurLine(L117-254):从 (x0,y0) 到 (x1,y1) 单调走格。
67	   * dx/dy ∈ {(±1,0),(0,±1)};行主序索引 idx = y*w+x。
68	   */
69	  private blurLine(x0: number, y0: number, x1: number, y1: number, dir: number): void {
70	    const { w, r, g, b, mask } = this;
71	    const dx = y0 === y1 ? dir : 0;
72	    const dy = x0 === x1 ? dir : 0;
73	    let x = x0, y = y0;
74	    let zr = 0, zg = 0, zb = 0;
75	    let dr = true, dg = true, dbb = true; // 死标记(true=已死)
76	    for (;;) {
77	      const i = y * w + x;
78	      const mr = r[i], mg = g[i], mb = b[i];
79	      // ① 源格判定 + 复活
80	      if (mr > zr) { zr = mr; dr = false; }
81	      if (mg > zg) { zg = mg; dg = false; }
82	      if (mb > zb) { zb = mb; dbb = false; }
83	      // ② 写入 / 死亡判定
84	      if (!dr) {
85	        if (zr < DEAD_THRESHOLD) dr = true;
86	        else if (mr <= zr) r[i] = zr;
87	      }
88	      if (!dg) {
89	        if (zg < DEAD_THRESHOLD) dg = true;
90	        else if (mg <= zg) g[i] = zg;
91	      }
92	      if (!dbb) {
93	        if (zb < DEAD_THRESHOLD) dbb = true;
94	        else if (mb <= zb) b[i] = zb;
95	      }
96	      // ③ 按当前格 mask 衰减(全通道死则跳过;L177-252)
97	      if (!dr || !dg || !dbb) {
98	        const m = mask[i];
99	        if (m === Mask.Solid) {
100	          if (!dr) zr *= DECAY_SOLID;
101	          if (!dg) zg *= DECAY_SOLID;
102	          if (!dbb) zb *= DECAY_SOLID;
103	        } else if (m === Mask.CrackedBricks) {
104	          if (!dr) zr *= DECAY_CRACKED;
105	          if (!dg) zg *= DECAY_CRACKED;
106	          if (!dbb) zb *= DECAY_CRACKED;
107	        } else if (m === Mask.Water) {
108	          // 逐格随机 [98,100)/100(L221-237)
109	          const flick = this.random.withModifier(i).next(98, 100) / 100;
110	          if (!dr) zr *= DECAY_WATER[0] * flick;
111	          if (!dg) zg *= DECAY_WATER[1] * flick;
112	          if (!dbb) zb *= DECAY_WATER[2] * flick;
113	        } else if (m === Mask.Honey) {
114	          if (!dr) zr *= DECAY_HONEY[0];
115	          if (!dg) zg *= DECAY_HONEY[1];
116	          if (!dbb) zb *= DECAY_HONEY[2];
117	        } else {
118	          if (!dr) zr *= DECAY_AIR;
119	          if (!dg) zg *= DECAY_AIR;
120	          if (!dbb) zb *= DECAY_AIR;
121	        }
122	      }
123	      if (x === x1 && y === y1) break;
124	      x += dx; y += dy;
125	    }
126	  }
127	}
128	

```

23	
24	/**

26	 * - 126 迪斯科球：frameX<36 → Disco RGB（TileLightScanner.cs:3057）
27	 * - 429 导线灯泡：frameX/18 位段 → 各线色 +0.5、致动器 +0.2R/G（cs:881-905）
28	 * - 209 传送门炮：frameX 234/252（紫）306/324（橙）×0.65（cs:432，PortalHelper 色近似）
29	 * - 84 开花药草：style2 死亡草随闪烁暗红 / style5 火焰草橙 / style6 颤骨草淡蓝（cs:3009）
30	 */
31	export function specialTileLight(
32	  sheet: number, frameX: number, frameY: number, flicker: FlickerClock,
33	): [number, number, number] | null {
34	  switch (sheet) {
35	    case 42: {
36	      // 灯笼(TileLightScanner 主 switch case 42):frameX!=0(灭档 18)不发光;
37	      // 光色按样式行 frameY/36 —— def.light 静态单色由 lantern def 移除,全走此表

39	      const LANTERN_LIGHT: Record<number, [number, number, number]> = {
40	        0: [0.7, 0.65, 0.55], 1: [0.9, 0.75, 0.6], 2: [0.8, 0.6, 0.6],
41	        3: [0.65, 0.5, 0.2], 4: [0.5, 0.7, 0.4], 5: [0.9, 0.4, 0.2],
42	        6: [0.7, 0.75, 0.3], 8: [0.75, 0.6, 0.3],
43	        // 7/9 = demonTorch 调制档，见下方动态分支
44	        11: [0.85, 0.6, 1], 14: [1, 0.95, 0.65],

48	      // 周期 200t）：style7 cs:2638-2645 R=G=0.9-d*0.2、B=0.7+d*0.2；

95	
96	export class TileLightScanner {
97	  private blocksLight: Uint8Array;
98	  private random = new FastRandom(0x9e3779b9);
99	  /** 每格局部随机(WithModifier(x,y);蜡烛闪烁) */
100	  private localRandom(x: number, y: number): FastRandom {
101	    return this.random.withModifier((y * 2654435761 + x) | 0);
102	  }
103	
104	  constructor(private flicker: FlickerClock) {
105	    // 从 TILE_DEFS 派生 tileBlockLight 近似(原版 Main.tileBlockLight[]):
106	    // 实心且近乎不透明才挡光——门(0.05)/平台(0.15)/树(0.1)/装饰(0.05)均不挡
107	    this.blocksLight = new Uint8Array(TILE_DEFS.length);
108	    TILE_DEFS.forEach((d, i) => {
109	      if (d && d.solid && !d.platform && d.opacity >= 0.9) this.blocksLight[i] = 1;
110	    });
111	  }
112	
113	  /** GetTileMask(:68-87) */
114	  getMask(st: TileStore, i: number): Mask {
115	    const type = st.type[i];
116	    const actuated = (st.wire[i] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) !== 0;
117	    if (type !== 0 && !actuated && this.blocksLight[type] && st.slope[i] === 0) {
118	      // 注意 actuated 已在上条件排除(原版 !tile.inActive())
119	      const sheet = TILE_DEFS[type]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
120	      return CRACKED_SHEETS.has(sheet) ? Mask.CrackedBricks : Mask.Solid;
121	    }
122	    if (st.liquidType[i] !== 2 && st.liquid[i] > 128) { // 岩浆 mask=None 只发光
123	      return st.liquidType[i] === 3 ? Mask.Honey : Mask.Water;
124	    }
125	    return Mask.None;
126	  }
127	
128	  /** 该格是否暴露于"天空/地狱光"(ApplySurfaceLight :3170 / ApplyHellLight :3270
129	   *  同构判定)。放行 = 满足下列任一 + 墙透光：
130	   *    ① 非活动(空气/水) 或 非挡阳类型；② 坡面/半砖 且【四邻液量全为 0】
131	   *  ——挡阳实心格（海底泥沙等）恒不放行：深水下海底不播天光，亮度只经水层
132	   *  传播衰减而来。此前误植为"自身 liquid>=200 才挡"= 海底方块自身无液被放行
133	   *  → 水底地面满天光自发光 */
134	  private exposed(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
135	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
136	    const type = st.type[i];
137	    if (type !== 0) {
138	      const actuated = (st.wire[i] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) !== 0;
139	      // AllowLightInWater（TileID.cs:359：玻璃 54/回声 541/彩纸 328/降雪 459/
140	      // 水族馆 748/尖刺 750）——水中也透天光（:3161 独立分支）
141	      const sheet = TILE_DEFS[type]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
142	      if (sheet === 54 || sheet === 541 || sheet === 328 || sheet === 459 || sheet === 748 || sheet === 750) {
143	        // 落到墙透光判定（原版此分支同样要求 wallLight）
144	      } else if (!actuated && this.blocksLight[type]) {
145	        if (st.slope[i] === 0 && st.half[i] === 0) return false;
146	        // 坡面/半砖例外：四邻必须全干（:3170 第三析取支的邻格 liquid==0）
147	        if (st.liquid[st.idx(x, y - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(x, y + 1)] !== 0
148	          || st.liquid[st.idx(x - 1, y)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(x + 1, y)] !== 0) return false;
149	      }
150	    }
151	    const wall = st.wall[i];
152	    return wall === 0 || WALL_LIGHT.has(wall);
153	  }
154	
155	  /** ExportTo(:17-43)+ GetTileLight(:89-108):向工作图播种 mask 与颜色 */
156	  exportTo(w: ScannerWorld, map: LightMap, rx: number, ry: number, perFrame: PerFrameLight[]): void {
157	    const st = w.store;
158	    map.clear();
159	    const [skyR, skyG, skyB] = skySeed(w.clock.timeOfDay, w.clock.dayCount, !!w.clock.eclipse);
160	    // 地狱脉动(ApplyHellLight:3266-3271)
161	    const hellV = 0.55 + Math.sin(performance.now() * 0.002) * 0.08; // GlobalTimeWrappedHourly 近似
162	    const hellR = hellV, hellG = hellV * 0.6, hellB = hellV * 0.2;
163	    // 岩浆闪烁基础(ApplyLiquidLight:118-131)
164	    const lavaV = 0.55 + (270 - this.flicker.mouseTextColor) / 900;
165	    const osc = this.flicker.mouseTextColor;
166	
167	    for (let ly = 0; ly < map.h; ly++) {
168	      const ty = ry + ly;
169	      for (let lx = 0; lx < map.w; lx++) {
170	        const tx = rx + lx;
171	        if (tx < 1 || ty < 1 || tx >= st.w - 1 || ty >= st.h - 1) continue; // InWorld(x,y,1)
172	        const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
173	        const li = ly * map.w + lx;
174	        map.mask[li] = this.getMask(st, i);
175	
176	        let lr = 0, lg = 0, lb = 0;
177	        // ★ 深度门 liquid<200（ApplySurfaceLight :3172 / ApplyHellLight :3270）：
178	        //   仅液量 <200（表面 ~4/5 格）播满天空/地狱光；深层液体不播——亮度靠
179	        //   表层向深水传播（水衰减逐格 0.88×0.91 系）→ 水体越深越暗。此前漏此门
180	        //   整个水柱每层播满 = 深水自发光
181	        const shallowLiquid = st.liquid[i] < 200;
182	        // ① 天空 / 地狱(:94-101)
183	        if (ty <= w.groundLevel) {
184	          if (shallowLiquid && this.exposed(st, tx, ty)) { lr = skyR; lg = skyG; lb = skyB; }
185	        } else if (ty > w.lavaLine) {
186	          if (shallowLiquid && this.exposed(st, tx, ty)) { lr = Math.max(lr, hellR); lg = Math.max(lg, hellG); lb = Math.max(lb, hellB); }
187	        }
188	        // ② 发光墙(:102;大多要求 !LightIsBlocked——此处墙在实心块后不可见,实心格直接跳过)
189	        const wall = st.wall[i];
190	        if (wall !== 0 && st.type[i] === 0) {
191	          const we = WALL_EMISSIVE[wall];
192	          if (we) { lr = Math.max(lr, we[0]); lg = Math.max(lg, we[1]); lb = Math.max(lb, we[2]); }
193	          // 彩色玻璃墙彩光（ApplySurfaceLight :3190-3242）：墙 88-93（紫/黄/蓝/绿/
194	          // 红/彩虹玻璃）/241 橙——格式满光后按墙种 RGB 乘区染色（switch wall-88 档）
195	          if ((wall >= 88 && wall <= 93) || wall === 241) {
196	            const k = wall === 241 ? 6 : wall - 88;
197	            const TINT: Array<[number, number, number]> = [
198	              [0.9, 0.15, 0.9], [0.9, 0.9, 0.15], [0.15, 0.15, 0.9], [0.15, 0.9, 0.15],
199	              [0.9, 0.15, 0.15], [0.7 + 0.2, 0.7, 0.7], [0.9, 0.5, 0],
200	            ];
201	            // 原版 switch：0 紫(0.9,0.15,0.9) 1 黄(0.9,0.9,0.15) 2 蓝(0.15,0.15,0.9)
202	            // 3 绿(0.15,0.9,0.15) 4 红(0.9,0.15,0.15) 5 彩虹(0.7+Disco×0.2) 6 橙(0.9,0.5,0)
203	            const dc = this.flicker.discoColor();  // Main.Disco 轮转（:3229-3233 彩虹档）
204	            const t = k === 5
205	              ? [0.7 + (dc[0] / 255) * 0.2, 0.7 + (dc[1] / 255) * 0.2, 0.7 + (dc[2] / 255) * 0.2]
206	              : TINT[k];
207	            // 此分支原版作用在天空光之上（num=num4 系）且要求 tile 不挡阳/液<255：
208	            // 此处以 max 合成等效（墙格无挡阳块时天空已满 → 乘区改写）
209	            lr = Math.max(lr, t[0]); lg = Math.max(lg, t[1]); lb = Math.max(lb, t[2]);
210	          }
211	        }
212	        // ③ 发光 tile(:103-106)
213	        const type = st.type[i];
214	        if (type !== 0 && (st.wire[i] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) === 0) {
215	          const d = TILE_DEFS[type];
216	          const sheet = d?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
217	          // 蘑菇雕像 349 门控(:2940):frameX<36 的 style 完全不发光——
218	          // 同时压制组公式与 def.light 静态兜底,故提到入口处判
219	          const shroomGateOk = !(sheet === 349 && st.frameX[i] < 36);
220	          if (d && (d.light || MUSHROOM_GLOW_SHEETS.has(sheet)) && shroomGateOk) {
221	            // 接线灯门控:关帧不发光(LIGHT_TOGGLE)
222	            const on = sheet < 0 || lightIsOn(sheet, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i]);
223	            if (on) {
224	              let er: number, eg: number, eb: number;
225	              if (sheet === 4) {
226	                // 火把:24 色动态(TorchID;frameY/22 = style)
227	                const style = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 22) % 24;
228	                if (style === 7) [er, eg, eb] = this.flicker.demonTorchColor();
229	                else if (style === 14) [er, eg, eb] = this.flicker.discoColor();
230	                else if (style === 23) [er, eg, eb] = this.flicker.shimmerTorchColor();
231	                else {
232	                  const c = TORCH_COLORS[style] ?? TORCH_COLORS[0];
233	                  [er, eg, eb] = c;
234	                }
235	                // 蜡烛/篝火族闪烁(:1034-1036:localRand.Next(28,42)*0.005+(270-mouseTextColor)/700)
236	                const f = this.localRandom(tx, ty).next(28, 42) * 0.005 + (270 - osc) / 700;
237	                er += f; eg += f; eb += f;
238	              } else if (MUSHROOM_GLOW_SHEETS.has(sheet)) {
239	                // 蘑菇组发光（TileLightScanner.cs:2938-2960 case 70/71/72/190/348/349/528/578）：
240	                //   R=0、B=1，G=0.2+num11/2；num11=Next(28,42)*0.005+(270-mouseTextColor)/1000
241	                //   ——只抬 G（与 FLICKER_SHEETS 的三通道叠加不同）
242	                const num11 = this.localRandom(tx, ty).next(28, 42) * 0.005 + (270 - osc) / 1000;
243	                er = 0; eg = 0.2 + num11 / 2; eb = 1;
244	              } else {
245	                er = d.light![0] / 255; eg = d.light![1] / 255; eb = d.light![2] / 255;
246	                if (FLICKER_SHEETS.has(sheet)) {
247	                  const f = this.localRandom(tx, ty).next(28, 42) * 0.005 + (270 - osc) / 700;
248	                  er += f; eg += f; eb += f;
249	                }
250	              }
251	              lr = Math.max(lr, er); lg = Math.max(lg, eg); lb = Math.max(lb, eb);
252	            }
253	          }
254	          // ③b 动态发光特例（原版 ApplyTileLight 帧条件/动态色 case，def.light 静态表无法表达）
255	          const spSheet = d?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
256	          const sp = specialTileLight(spSheet, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i], this.flicker);
257	          if (sp) {
258	            lr = Math.max(lr, sp[0]); lg = Math.max(lg, sp[1]); lb = Math.max(lb, sp[2]);
259	          }
260	        }
261	        // ④ 液体光(:107;岩浆发光,水/蜂蜜不发光)
262	        if (st.liquid[i] > 0 && st.liquidType[i] === 2) {
263	          lr = Math.max(lr, lavaV); lg = Math.max(lg, lavaV * 0.6); lb = Math.max(lb, lavaV * 0.2);
264	        }
265	        map.r[li] = lr; map.g[li] = lg; map.b[li] = lb;
266	      }
267	    }
268	    // ⑤ 动态点光(ApplyPerFrameLights:204-227;区域内 max 合并)
269	    for (const l of perFrame) {
270	      const lx = l.x - rx, ly = l.y - ry;
271	      if (lx < 0 || ly < 0 || lx >= map.w || ly >= map.h) continue;
272	      const li = ly * map.w + lx;
273	      if (l.r > map.r[li]) map.r[li] = l.r;
274	      if (l.g > map.g[li]) map.g[li] = l.g;
275	      if (l.b > map.b[li]) map.b[li] = l.b;
276	    }
277	  }
278	}

1	// 天空色与全局闪烁驱动(纯函数,Main.cs 数值一比一)。
2	// 我们的 Clock:timeOfDay 0-1(0=午夜 0.5=正午),isDay=(0.25,0.75)。
3	// 原版 Main.time:昼 0-54000(4:30-18:00),夜 54000-86400。此处做时间映射,
4	// 不改 Clock 本身(SkyRenderer/音频仍吃 World.dayFactor)。
5	import { MOON_FLOOR } from './lightTables';
6	
7	/** timeOfDay → 原版 Main.time(0-86400) */
8	export function toVanillaTime(timeOfDay: number, isDay: boolean): number {
9	  if (isDay) {
10	    // 0.25(6:00 日出边界)→0,0.75(18:00)→54000,向两端外延钳制
11	    const p = (timeOfDay - 0.25) / 0.5;
12	    return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, p)) * 54000;
13	  }
14	  const p = ((timeOfDay - 0.75 + 1) % 1) / 0.5;
15	  return 54000 + Math.max(0, Math.min(1, p)) * 32400;
16	}
17	
18	/** 月相 0-7(Main.cs:64880:每黎明 +1 mod 8;dayCount 从 1 起,首夜相位 0) */
19	export function moonPhase(dayCount: number): number {
20	  return Math.max(0, dayCount) % 8;
21	}
22	
23	/** SetBackColor 五段昼夜曲线(Main.cs:62889-63362 主体)→ [R,G,B] 0-255 */
24	export function setBackColor(mainTime: number, isDay: boolean, phase: number): [number, number, number] {
25	  let r: number, g: number, b: number;
26	  if (isDay) {
27	    if (mainTime < 13500) {
28	      // 黎明(4:30-7:30):暖橙 → 白
29	      const p = mainTime / 13500;
30	      r = 230 * p + 25; g = 220 * p + 35; b = 220 * p + 35;
31	    } else if (mainTime > 37800 && mainTime <= 45900) {
32	      // 黄昏前段(15:15-17:45):白 → 橙
33	      const p = 1 - (mainTime / 54000 - 0.7) * 6.666666666666667;
34	      r = 20 * p + 235; g = 135 * p + 120; b = 85 * p + 170;
35	    } else if (mainTime > 45900) {
36	      // 黄昏后段(17:45-18:00)→ 夜前
37	      const p = 1 - (mainTime / 54000 - 0.85) * 6.666666666666667;
38	      r = 200 * p + 35; g = 85 * p + 35; b = 135 * p + 35;
39	    } else {
40	      r = 255; g = 255; b = 255; // 正午全亮
41	    }
42	  } else {
43	    const t = mainTime - 54000;
44	    if (t < 16200) {
45	      // 入夜(18:00-23:00)
46	      const p = 1 - t / 16200;
47	      r = 30 * p + 5; g = 30 * p + 5; b = 30 * p + 5;
48	    } else {
49	      // 深夜(23:00-4:30)
50	      const p = (t / 32400 - 0.5) * 2;
51	      r = 20 * p + 5; g = 30 * p + 5; b = 30 * p + 5;
52	    }
53	  }
54	  // 月相地板(Main.cs:63230-63266):每通道下限
55	  const floor = MOON_FLOOR[phase % 8];
56	  r = Math.max(r, floor); g = Math.max(g, floor); b = Math.max(b, floor);
57	  return [Math.min(255, r), Math.min(255, g), Math.min(255, b)];
58	}
59	
60	/** tileColor(Main.cs:62608-62616):天空色 → 地表光种子(0-1 RGB)。
61	 *  tileColor.C = (R+G+B + 7C)/10:向本通道倾斜的加权均值 */
62	export function tileColor(bg: readonly [number, number, number]): [number, number, number] {
63	  const sum = bg[0] + bg[1] + bg[2];
64	  return [
65	    (sum + 7 * bg[0]) / 10 / 255,
66	    (sum + 7 * bg[1]) / 10 / 255,
67	    (sum + 7 * bg[2]) / 10 / 255,
68	  ];
69	}
70	
71	/** 一次调用得到天空光种子(0-1 RGB)。eclipse=true 时按 Main.cs:63282-63305 压暗：
72	 *  eclipseLight = min(1, 白天 Main.time/1242)（约 25 游戏秒到满暗）；R×(1-0.925L)
73	 *  G×(1-0.96L) B×(1-1.0L)——蓝色通道清零、红色保留 7.5% 的血红暮色 */
74	export function skySeed(timeOfDay: number, dayCount: number, eclipse = false): [number, number, number] {
75	  const isDay = timeOfDay > 0.25 && timeOfDay < 0.75;
76	  const t = toVanillaTime(timeOfDay, isDay);
77	  let bg = setBackColor(t, isDay, moonPhase(dayCount));
78	  if (eclipse && isDay) {
79	    const L = Math.min(1, t / 1242);
80	    bg = [
81	      bg[0] * (1 - 0.925 * L),
82	      bg[1] * (1 - 0.96 * L),
83	      bg[2] * (1 - 1 * L),
84	    ];
85	  }
86	  return tileColor(bg);
87	}
88	
89	// ---- 全局闪烁驱动(火把动态时钟源,由引擎每 tick 推进) ----
90	// 四态全部封装在一个小状态机里,等价原版 Main.CursorColor(51896-51905)+
91	// DoUpdate_AnimateCursorColors(18064-18076)/DoUpdate_AnimateTileGlows(18087-18101)/
92	// DoUpdate_AnimateDiscoRGB(19442-19502)。
93	export class FlickerClock {
94	  /** mouseTextColor:190↔255 步进 1/帧(字节环绕) */
95	  mouseTextColor = 255;
96	  private mouseDir = -1;
97	  /** cursorAlpha(Main.cs:51897-51904):0.6↔1 步进 0.015/帧,驱动光标/心/星呼吸 */
98	  cursorAlpha = 1;
99	  private cursorDir = -1;
100	  /** demonTorch:0↔1 步进 0.01/帧 */
101	  demonTorch = 0;
102	  private demonDir = 1;
103	  /** Disco RGB:6 相循环,每通道步进 7/帧(0-255) */
104	  discoR = 255; discoG = 0; discoB = 0;
105	  private discoStyle = 0;
106	
107	  /** cursorScale(Main.cs:51905):= cursorAlpha*0.3 + 0.8,资源条 flag 心/星缩放脉冲源 */
108	  get cursorScale(): number { return this.cursorAlpha * 0.3 + 0.8; }
109	
110	  tick(): void {
111	    this.cursorAlpha += this.cursorDir * 0.015;
112	    if (this.cursorAlpha >= 1) { this.cursorAlpha = 1; this.cursorDir = -1; }
113	    else if (this.cursorAlpha <= 0.6) { this.cursorAlpha = 0.6; this.cursorDir = 1; }
114	
115	    this.mouseTextColor += this.mouseDir;
116	    if (this.mouseTextColor >= 255) this.mouseDir = -1;
117	    else if (this.mouseTextColor <= 190) this.mouseDir = 1;
118	
119	    this.demonTorch += this.demonDir * 0.01;
120	    if (this.demonTorch > 1) { this.demonTorch = 1; this.demonDir = -1; }
121	    else if (this.demonTorch < 0) { this.demonTorch = 0; this.demonDir = 1; }
122	
123	    // 6 相:G↑ → R↓ → B↑ → G↓ → R↑ → B↓(每帧步进 7,满额切换下一相)
124	    const STEP = 7;
125	    switch (this.discoStyle) {
126	      case 0: this.discoG += STEP; if (this.discoG >= 255) { this.discoG = 255; this.discoStyle = 1; } break;
127	      case 1: this.discoR -= STEP; if (this.discoR <= 0) { this.discoR = 0; this.discoStyle = 2; } break;
128	      case 2: this.discoB += STEP; if (this.discoB >= 255) { this.discoB = 255; this.discoStyle = 3; } break;
129	      case 3: this.discoG -= STEP; if (this.discoG <= 0) { this.discoG = 0; this.discoStyle = 4; } break;
130	      case 4: this.discoR += STEP; if (this.discoR >= 255) { this.discoR = 255; this.discoStyle = 5; } break;
131	      case 5: this.discoB -= STEP; if (this.discoB <= 0) { this.discoB = 0; this.discoStyle = 0; } break;
132	    }
133	  }
134	
135	  /** 恶魔火把色插值(TorchID DemonTorchLight:37-45) */
136	  demonTorchColor(): [number, number, number] {
137	    const d = this.demonTorch;
138	    return [0.5 * d + 1 * (1 - d), 0.3, 1 * d + 0.5 * (1 - d)];
139	  }
140	
141	  /** 微光火把色(TorchID ShimmerTorchLight:48-62;num/num2 由 mouseTextColor 派生钳 0-1) */
142	  shimmerTorchColor(): [number, number, number] {
143	    const num = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (this.mouseTextColor - 190) / 65));
144	    const num2 = 1 - num;
145	    return [num * 0.9, num2 * 0.55, num * 1.2];
146	  }
147	
148	  discoColor(): [number, number, number] {
149	    return [this.discoR / 255, this.discoG / 255, this.discoB / 255];
150	  }
151	}

1	// Java Random 克隆(Terrarria1456/Terraria.Utilities/FastRandom.cs:7-88 一比一)。
2	// 48 位 LCG:seed = (seed*25214903917 + 11) & (2^48-1);Next(bits) = seed >> (48-bits)。
3	// 用于水波纹逐格随机(LightMap.Blur)与蜡烛/篝火闪烁(TileLightScanner)。
4	// 注意：本文件是光照系统专用副本——世界生成侧（DesertHive 等）请用
5	// src/core/rng.ts 的 FastRandom（同一原版类，带 Next(max)/NextDouble/WithModifier(x,y)）。
6	const MULTIPLIER = 25214903917n;
7	const ADD = 11n;
8	const MASK = 0xffffffffffffn; // 2^48 - 1
9	
10	export class FastRandom {
11	  private seed: bigint;
12	
13	  constructor(seed: bigint | number) {
14	    this.seed = (typeof seed === 'bigint' ? seed : BigInt(seed)) & MASK;
15	  }
16	
17	  /** NextSeed(L85-88) */
18	  static nextSeed(seed: bigint): bigint {
19	    return (seed * MULTIPLIER + ADD) & MASK;
20	  }
21	
22	  /** WithModifier(m)(L27-30):新实例种子 = NextSeed(m) ^ 原 Seed */
23	  withModifier(m: number | bigint): FastRandom {
24	    const mod = typeof m === 'bigint' ? m : BigInt(m);
25	    return new FastRandom(FastRandom.nextSeed(mod) ^ this.seed);
26	  }
27	
28	  /** NextBits(b)(L47-51):前进一步并取高 b 位 */
29	  private nextBits(bits: number): number {
30	    this.seed = FastRandom.nextSeed(this.seed);
31	    return Number(this.seed >> BigInt(48 - bits));
32	  }
33	
34	  /** Next(31) → int */
35	  nextInt31(): number {
36	    return this.nextBits(31);
37	  }
38	
39	  /** Next(min,max)(L63-83):Next(range)+min;拒绝采样避免模偏置。
40	   *  原版用 int32 溢出判定(num - num2 + (max-1) < 0);JS 无溢出,用等价的
41	   *  Math.ceil(range/2) 上界判据:当 num 落在 int32 顶部的溢出带时重投 */
42	  next(min: number, max: number): number {
43	    const range = max - min;
44	    if (range <= 0) return min;
45	    return this.nextBelow(range) + min;
46	  }
47	
48	  /** Next(max)(L68-82);max 为 2 的幂走快速路径 */
49	  private nextBelow(max: number): number {
50	    if ((max & -max) === max) {
51	      // (max * NextBits(31)) >> 31
52	      return Math.floor((max * this.nextBits(31)) / 2147483648);
53	    }
54	    for (;;) {
55	      const num = this.nextBits(31);          // [0, 2^31)
56	      const rem = num % max;
57	      // 原版溢出带:num - rem + (max-1) 溢出 int32 < 0
58	      //   等价:num > 2^31 - 1 - (max - 1),即 num >= 2^31 - max + ... 顶部 max-1 个值
59	      if (num <= 2147483647 - (max - 1)) return rem;
60	    }
61	  }
62	
63	  /** NextFloat() */
64	  nextFloat(): number {
65	    return this.nextBits(24) / 16777216;
66	  }
67	}
68

8	namespace Terraria.Graphics.Light;
9	
10	public class LightingEngine : ILightingEngine
11	{
12		private enum EngineState
13		{
14			MinimapUpdate,
15			ExportMetrics,
16			Scan,
17			Blur,
18			Max
19		}
20

22		{

24	
25			public readonly Vector3 Color = color;
26		}

45

47	
48		public void AddLight(int x, int y, Vector3 color)
49		{
50			_perFrameLights.Add(new PerFrameLight(new Point(x, y), color));
51		}
52	
53		public void Clear()
54		{
55			_activeLightMap.Clear();
56			_workingLightMap.Clear();
57			_perFrameLights.Clear();
58			_oldPerFrameLights.Clear();
59		}
60	
61		public Vector3 GetColor(int x, int y)
62		{
63			if (!_activeProcessedArea.Contains(x, y))
64			{
65				return Vector3.Zero;
66			}
67			x -= _activeProcessedArea.X;
68			y -= _activeProcessedArea.Y;
69			return _activeLightMap[x, y];
70		}
71	
72		public void ProcessArea(Rectangle area)
73		{
74			TimeLogger.StartTimestamp fromTimestamp = TimeLogger.Start();
75			switch (_state)
76			{
77			case EngineState.MinimapUpdate:
78				if (Main.mapDelay > 0)
79				{
80					Main.mapDelay--;
81				}
82				else
83				{
84					ExportToMiniMap();
85				}
86				Main.renderCount = 3;
87				break;
88			case EngineState.ExportMetrics:
89				Main.UpdateSceneMetrics();
90				Main.renderCount = 0;
91				break;
92			case EngineState.Scan:
93				ProcessScan(area);
94				Main.renderCount = 1;
95				break;
96			case EngineState.Blur:
97				ProcessBlur();
98				Present();
99				Main.renderCount = 2;
100				break;
101			}
102			TimeLogger.LightingByPass[(int)_state].AddTime(fromTimestamp);
103			IncrementState();
104		}
105	
106		private void IncrementState()
107		{
108			_state = (EngineState)((int)(_state + 1) % 4);
109		}
110	
111		private void ProcessScan(Rectangle area)
112		{
113			area.Inflate(28, 28);
114			_workingProcessedArea = area;
115			_workingLightMap.SetSize(area.Width, area.Height);
116			_workingLightMap.NonVisiblePadding = 18;
117			_tileScanner.Update();
118			_tileScanner.ExportTo(area, _workingLightMap, new TileLightScannerOptions
119			{
120				DrawInvisibleWalls = Main.ShouldShowInvisibleBlocksAndWalls()
121			});
122		}
123	
124		private void ProcessBlur()

126			UpdateLightDecay();
127			ApplyPerFrameLights();
128			_workingLightMap.Blur();
129		}
130	
131		private void Present()
132		{
133			Utils.Swap(ref _activeLightMap, ref _workingLightMap);
134			Utils.Swap(ref _activeProcessedArea, ref _workingProcessedArea);
135		}
136	
137		private void UpdateLightDecay()
138		{
139			LightMap workingLightMap = _workingLightMap;
140			workingLightMap.LightDecayThroughAir = 0.91f;
141			workingLightMap.LightDecayThroughSolid = 0.56f;
142			workingLightMap.LightDecayThroughHoney = new Vector3(0.75f, 0.7f, 0.6f) * 0.91f;
143			switch (Main.waterStyle)
144			{
145			case 0:
146			case 1:
147			case 7:
148			case 8:
149				workingLightMap.LightDecayThroughWater = new Vector3(0.88f, 0.96f, 1.015f) * 0.91f;
150				break;
151			case 2:
152				workingLightMap.LightDecayThroughWater = new Vector3(0.94f, 0.85f, 1.01f) * 0.91f;
153				break;
154			case 3:
155				workingLightMap.LightDecayThroughWater = new Vector3(0.84f, 0.95f, 1.015f) * 0.91f;

204		private void ApplyPerFrameLights()
205		{
206			List<PerFrameLight> list = _perFrameLights;
207			if (Main.gamePaused)
208			{
209				list = _oldPerFrameLights;

215				{

217					Vector3 value2 = _workingLightMap[position.X - _workingProcessedArea.X, position.Y - _workingProcessedArea.Y];
218					Vector3.Max(ref value2, ref value, out value);
219					_workingLightMap[position.X - _workingProcessedArea.X, position.Y - _workingProcessedArea.Y] = value;
220				}
221			}
222			if (!CaptureManager.Instance.IsCapturing && !Main.gamePaused)
223			{
224				Utils.Swap(ref _perFrameLights, ref _oldPerFrameLights);
225				_perFrameLights.Clear();
226			}
227		}

245			}
246			Rectangle area = new Rectangle(_activeProcessedArea.X + 28, _activeProcessedArea.Y + 28, _activeProcessedArea.Width - 56, _activeProcessedArea.Height - 56);
247			Rectangle value = new Rectangle(0, 0, Main.maxTilesX, Main.maxTilesY);
248			value.Inflate(-40, -40);
249			area = Rectangle.Intersect(area, value);
250			area = Rectangle.Intersect(area, MapHelper.sceneArea);
251			FastParallel.For(area.Left, area.Right, (ParallelForAction)delegate(int start, int end, object context)
252			{
253				for (int i = start; i < end; i++)
254				{
255					for (int j = area.Top; j < area.Bottom; j++)
256					{
257						Vector3 vector = _activeLightMap[i - _activeProcessedArea.X, j - _activeProcessedArea.Y];
258						float num = Math.Max(Math.Max(vector.X, vector.Y), vector.Z);
259						byte light = (byte)Math.Min(255, (int)(num * 255f));
260						Main.Map.UpdateLighting(i, j, light);
261					}
262				}
263			}, (object)null);
264			Main.updateMap = area;
265		}

35	export function updateLightDecay(waterStyle: number, nightVisionOn: boolean, blackoutOn = false): void {
36	  nightVision = nightVisionOn;
37	  const base = WATER_DECAY_BY_STYLE[Math.max(0, Math.min(13, waterStyle))];
38	  DECAY_WATER = [base[0] * 0.91, base[1] * 0.91, base[2] * 0.91];

40	  const blk = blackoutOn ? 0.85 : 1;
41	  DECAY_AIR = 0.91 * (nightVision ? NIGHT_VISION_MUL : 1) * blk;
42	  DECAY_SOLID = 0.56 * (nightVision ? NIGHT_VISION_MUL : 1) * blk;
43	  // 原版另有 blind ×0.95 / headcovered ×0.85 与 SceneState 调制——对应系统未实装,跳过
44	}

5	public struct FastRandom
6	{
7		private const ulong RANDOM_MULTIPLIER = 25214903917uL;
8	
9		private const ulong RANDOM_ADD = 11uL;
10	
11		private const ulong RANDOM_MASK = 281474976710655uL;
12	
13		public ulong Seed { get; private set; }
14	
15		public FastRandom(ulong seed)
16		{
17			this = default(FastRandom);
18			Seed = seed;
19		}
20	
21		public FastRandom(int seed)
22		{
23			this = default(FastRandom);
24			Seed = (ulong)seed;
25		}
26	
27		public FastRandom WithModifier(ulong modifier)
28		{
29			return new FastRandom(NextSeed(modifier) ^ Seed);
30		}
31	
32		public FastRandom WithModifier(int x, int y)
33		{
34			return WithModifier((ulong)(x + 2654435769u + ((long)y << 6)) + ((ulong)y >> 2));
35		}
36	
37		public static FastRandom CreateWithRandomSeed()
38		{
39			return new FastRandom((ulong)Guid.NewGuid().GetHashCode());
40		}
41	
42		public void NextSeed()
43		{
44			Seed = NextSeed(Seed);
45		}
46	
47		private int NextBits(int bits)
48		{
49			Seed = NextSeed(Seed);
50			return (int)(Seed >> 48 - bits);
51		}
52	
53		public float NextFloat()
54		{
55			return (float)NextBits(24) * 5.9604645E-08f;
56		}
57	
58		public double NextDouble()
59		{
60			return (float)NextBits(32) * 4.656613E-10f;
61		}
62	
63		public int Next(int max)
64		{
65			if ((max & -max) == max)
66			{
67				return (int)((long)max * (long)NextBits(31) >> 31);
68			}
69			int num;
70			int num2;
71			do
72			{
73				num = NextBits(31);
74				num2 = num % max;
75			}
76			while (num - num2 + (max - 1) < 0);
77			return num2;
78		}
79	
80		public int Next(int min, int max)
81		{
82			return Next(max - min) + min;
83		}
84	
85		private static ulong NextSeed(ulong seed)
86		{
87			return (seed * 25214903917L + 11) & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFL;
88		}
89	}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T06:16:46.382Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
Key findings accumulating.

**FastRandom withModifier(x, y):** Original:
```csharp
return WithModifier((ulong)(x + 2654435769u + ((long)y << 6)) + ((ulong)y >> 2));
```
Repo TileLightScanner.localRandom:
```ts
return this.random.withModifier((y * 2654435761 + x) | 0);
```
That's a DIFFERENT hash! Original is `x + 2654435769 + (y<<6) + (y>>2)` — Knuth-ish. Repo uses `y * 2654435761 + x`. Both are valid 2D hashes but NOT 1:1. Since the base seed is random (CreateWithRandomSeed), per-cell flicker is not bit-identical anyway. But it's a deviation worth noting — random values differ from vanilla per cell (not deterministically comparable anyway due to random base seed). Note the original's `_random.NextSeed()` in Update() advances once per scan; repo's TileLightScanner.random is a fixed seed `0x9e3779b9` and never advances! Original: `_tileScanner.Update()` is called in ProcessScan which does `_random.NextSeed()` — advancing the base seed each scan, so the per-cell flicker changes every scan (every 4 ticks). The repo scanner random is FIXED at construction and never advanced → per-cell candle flicker value is CONSTANT across time (only the (270-mouseTextColor)/700 term varies). That's a real deviation: original re-rolls localRandom per scan so torches flicker with both random + slow wave; repo keeps the same random per cell forever → static per-cell offset + wave. Visual: less flicker. Let me verify repo has no NextSeed on scanner random — searching TileLightScanner.ts... `private random = new FastRandom(0x9e3779b9);` and only `withModifier` calls. Yes, never advanced. Deviation.

Also `_random` in original TileLightScanner is `CreateWithRandomSeed()` — random. Repo fixed seed. Minor.

**LightMap random:** original `_random.NextSeed()` after two BlurPasses → advances seed. Repo: `this.random = this.random.withModifier(++this.cycleIndex)` — that's `NextSeed(cycleIndex) ^ Seed` NOT `NextSeed(Seed)`. Different progression! Original NextSeed() → seed' = seed*M + 11. Repo withModifier(c) → seed' = (c*M + 11) ^ seed. Both produce a different seed per cycle, so the per-cell water flicker changes every cycle. Semantically equivalent in character (random), not bit-identical. Note as minor deviation.

**Water flicker modifier:** original `WithModifier((ulong)i)` where i is the raw index in the map (column-major `x*Height+y`), repo `withModifier(i)` with i = y*w+x (row-major). Different i → different values, but random anyway. Also note: original applies per-cell water random with SAME i for both BlurPasses within one Blur() call (seed only advanced after both passes), repo same (cycleIndex incremented after both passes). OK.

Hmm wait, actually there's a subtle bug-ish thing: in the original, `_random.WithModifier(i)` — i here is the *linear index*, same for both passes. In repo, same. Fine.

If NOT actuated (active stone): type==131 is InactiveStoneBlock placed as active... The carve-out means an ACTIVE InactiveStoneBlock (placed via actuators turned on→off) does not block light. Hmm — the repo's getMask lacks `type != 131`. Impact: active InactiveStoneBlock would be Mask.Solid in repo but Mask.None in vanilla. Edge case. Deviation (minor).

Wait — actually let me reconsider: In vanilla, tile.inActive() is the actuation state. InactiveStoneBlock is the actuator item's tile type. When placed and not actuated, it's a solid stone-like block that vanilla excludes from light blocking. Repo: blocksLight derived from TILE_DEFS solid+opacity → likely 1. So deviation. **ShimmerTorchLight:** vanilla:
```
num = 0.9 + (270-mouseTextColor)/900; clamped 0..1
num2 = 0.9 + (270-mouseTextColor)/125; clamped 0..1
r = num*0.9; g = num2*0.55; b = num*1.2
```
Repo shimmerTorchColor:
```
const num = clamp((mouseTextColor - 190)/65, 0, 1);
const num2 = 1 - num;
return [num*0.9, num2*0.55, num*1.2];
```
Completely different formula! Vanilla derives from (270 - mouseTextColor) with clamps and separate scaling for r (num*0.9) and b (num*1.2) where num = clamp(0.9 + (270-mt)/900). Note (270-mt)/900: mt∈[190,255] → (15..80)/900 = 0.0167..0.0889 → num = 0.917..0.989. num2 = 0.9 + (15..80)/125 = 0.9+0.12..0.64 = 1.02..1.54 → clamped to 1. So num2 = 1 always (since min is 1.02 > 1). So g = 0.55 constant; r = 0.825..0.89, b = 1.1..1.19.

Repo: num = (mt-190)/65 ∈ [0,1]; returns r = num*0.9 ∈[0,0.9], g=(1-num)*0.55, b=num*1.2. So the repo's shimmer torch oscillates r 0..0.9 and g 0.55..0 and b 0..1.2 with mouseTextColor; vanilla keeps shimmer torch nearly CONSTANT (r≈0.83-0.89, g=0.55, b≈1.1-1.19) with tiny ripple. Big deviation. using System;
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;
using Terraria.Graphics;
using Terraria.Graphics.Light;
using Terraria.ID;
using Terraria.Utilities;

namespace Terraria;

public class Lighting
{
	private const float DEFAULT_GLOBAL_BRIGHTNESS = 1.2f;

	private const float BLIND_GLOBAL_BRIGHTNESS = 1f;

	[Old]
	public static int OffScreenTiles = 45;

	private static LightMode _mode = LightMode.Color;

	private static readonly LightingEngine NewEngine = new LightingEngine();

	private static readonly LegacyLighting LegacyEngine = new LegacyLighting(Main.Camera);

	private static ILightingEngine _activeEngine;

	public static float GlobalBrightness { get; set; }

	public static LightMode Mode
	{
		get
		{
			return _mode;
		}
		set
		{
			_mode = value;
			switch (_mode)
			{
			case LightMode.Color:
				_activeEngine = NewEngine;
				LegacyEngine.Mode = 0;
				OffScreenTiles = 35;
				break;
			case LightMode.White:
				_activeEngine = LegacyEngine;
				LegacyEngine.Mode = 1;
				break;
			case LightMode.Retro:
				_activeEngine = LegacyEngine;
				LegacyEngine.Mode = 2;
				break;
			case LightMode.Trippy:
				_activeEngine = LegacyEngine;
				LegacyEngine.Mode = 3;
				break;
			}
			Main.renderCount = 0;
			Main.renderNow = false;
		}
	}

	public static bool NotRetro
	{
		get
		{
			if (Mode != LightMode.Retro)
			{
				return Mode != LightMode.Trippy;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

	public static bool UsingNewLighting => Mode == LightMode.Color;

	public static bool UpdateEveryFrame
	{
		get
		{
			if (!Main.RenderTargetsRequired)
			{
				return !NotRetro;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

	public static void Initialize()
	{
		GlobalBrightness = 1.2f;
		NewEngine.Rebuild();
		LegacyEngine.Rebuild();
		if (_activeEngine == null)
		{
			Mode = LightMode.Color;
		}
	}

	public static void LightTiles(Rectangle area)
	{
		TimeLogger.StartTimestamp fromTimestamp = TimeLogger.Start();
		Main.render = true;
		UpdateGlobalBrightness();
		_activeEngine.ProcessArea(area);
		TimeLogger.Lighting.AddTime(fromTimestamp);
	}

	private static void UpdateGlobalBrightness()
	{
		GlobalBrightness = 1.2f;
		if (Main.player[Main.myPlayer].blind)
		{
			GlobalBrightness = 1f;
		}
	}

	public static float Brightness(int x, int y)
	{
		Vector3 color = _activeEngine.GetColor(x, y);
		return GlobalBrightness * (color.X + color.Y + color.Z) / 3f;
	}

	public static Vector3 GetSubLight(Vector2 position)
	{
		Vector2 vector = position / 16f - new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
		Vector2 vector2 = new Vector2(vector.X % 1f, vector.Y % 1f);
		int num = (int)vector.X;
		int num2 = (int)vector.Y;
		Vector3 color = _activeEngine.GetColor(num, num2);
		Vector3 color2 = _activeEngine.GetColor(num + 1, num2);
		Vector3 color3 = _activeEngine.GetColor(num, num2 + 1);
		Vector3 color4 = _activeEngine.GetColor(num + 1, num2 + 1);
		Vector3 value = Vector3.Lerp(color, color2, vector2.X);
		Vector3 value2 = Vector3.Lerp(color3, color4, vector2.X);
		return Vector3.Lerp(value, value2, vector2.Y);
	}

	{
		Mode++;

        3002: [1.05, 0.95, 0.55], 4776: [0.9, 0.35, 1],
      };
      if (gvid === 5643) {
        const ph = Math.floor(this.tickCount / 10) % 6;
        heldRGB = ([[1, 0.2, 0.2], [1, 1, 0.2], [0.2, 1, 0.2], [0.2, 1, 1], [0.2, 0.2, 1], [1, 0.2, 1]] as Array<[number, number, number]>)[ph];
      } else if (gvid >= 0 && GLOWSTICK_LIGHT[gvid]) {

      const dst = this.world.store;
      if (!dst.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;

      if (isTorch || isCandle) {
        if (dst.liquid[dst.idx(tx, ty)] > 100) continue; // !wet

    for (const bucket of [this.entities.enemies, this.entities.critters, this.entities.projectiles, this.entities.drops]) {

  private drawHealthBar(e: { cx: number; cy: number; y: number; h: number; hp: number; maxHp: number; vanillaId: number | null; wormFollow?: Enemy['wormFollow']; spriteBottomWorld?: number }, expert = false) {

    if (hp <= 0 || hp >= maxHp) return;

    const x = e.cx - 18 * scale;

    const fill = Math.max(0, Math.min(36, Math.floor((36 * hp) / maxHp)));  // num2

Renderer consumption: a full-screen multiply pass. Light values are Uint8 (0-255, already ×1.2 and clamped in Present). Bilinear 2× supersampled. Then `multiply` composite. Semantically matches Lighting.GetColor (×1.2, clamp) × sprite color. Two notable deltas vs vanilla:
1. Interpolation: vanilla tiles are lit per-tile (no interpolation within a tile for the tile pass; GetColor9Slice is used for smooth lighting on slopes etc.). The repo applies bilinear interpolation across tiles to everything → smoother but not vanilla. Also note GetSubLight exists in vanilla for entities (bilinear at pixel level) — used by some draw paths. So interpolation for entities is arguably vanilla-ish; for tiles vanilla is flat per-tile. The repo's supersampling makes the tile pass smoother than vanilla. Deviation (visual smoothing).

OK, so the repo internally maps to vanilla-day-space and subtracts. Fine.

That matches if enum order is Empty, QuarterL, QuarterR, HalfL, HalfR, 3Q L, 3Q R, Full. Let me verify the MoonPhase enum ordering and GetMoonPhase.

Repo's moonPhase(dayCount) = dayCount % 8, with comment "Main.cs:64880: 每黎明 +1 mod 8; dayCount 从 1 起,首夜相位 0". Need to check vanilla: moonPhase computed as `GetMoonPhase()` = `(Main.moonPhase)`? Actually vanilla Main.moonPhase = (int)((Main.dayCount... )). Let me grep.

- Eclipse: repo eclipse L = min(1, t/1242) where t = vanilla Main.time (0..54000 day). Vanilla: eclipseLight = time/1242 clamped — and eclipseLight DECAYS by 0.01/frame after eclipse ends, and the darkening persists (eclipseLight>0 applies ×(1-0.925L) etc.). Repo: only applies while eclipse && isDay, L = t/1242 → at eclipse start (time≈0 at 4:30am) light is 0 and ramps to full dark by time=1242 (≈5:15am)... but an eclipse starts at dawn (4:30) — so the darkening ramps over ~45 in-game minutes. Hmm wait, time 1242/54000 of the day = ~5.6 min real time. Anyway repo matches the ramp formula, but missing the 0.01/frame decay tail (after eclipse ends, vanilla fades out over 100 frames). Also missing sunColor override and the min clamps (R≥20, G≥10). Repo's eclipse multipliers match: (1-0.925L, 1-0.96L, 1-1.0L). Good. Partial.

- Blood moon: missing both the night curve branch AND the min-25 floor. 缺失.

- Biome influences (Corruption/Crimson/Hallow?/Desert?/Mushroom/Jungle/Sky/ etc.) — repo missing entirely. Also cloudAlpha×atmo darkening, GraveyardInfluence ×(1-0.6g), lightning flash lerp-to-white, remixWorld → 1,1,1. Also AuroraSky.ModifyTileColor and HorizonRenderer.ModifyHorizonLight and SkyManager.ProcessTileColor(tileColor). All missing. Note: some of these affect ColorOfTheSkies → tileColor → sky seed. So the repo's sky seed is a simplified subset. But it's a huge surface; list the main ones.

Now, importantly: the repo's tileColor is applied from setBackColor(bg) via tileColor(bg) formula which matches ApplyColorOfTheSkiesToTiles: (R+G+B+7C)/10. ✓. But vanilla divides by 10 with integer math on 0-255 ints and casts to byte: `(byte)((sum + R*7)/10)` — integer division truncation. Repo: `(sum + 7*bg[0]) / 10 / 255` with float bg values — near-identical (vanilla truncates per channel; repo rounds via float). Negligible.

Note: vanilla `ColorOfTheSkies` is the value after all mods, and `SkyManager.Instance.ProcessTileColor(tileColor)` (e.g., during events like Tower Nebula etc.). Fine.

/** 该格灯光是否点亮(非灯器件恒 true;供 LightingEngine 查询) */
export function lightIsOn(sheet: number, frameX: number, frameY: number): boolean {
  const t = LIGHT_TOGGLE[sheet];
  if (!t) return true;
  const v = t[0] === 'x' ? frameX : frameY;
  return v % (2 * t[1]) < t[1];
}

/** HitWireSingle 器件主分发表。返回 true = 已处理 */
export function deviceTable(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, sheet: number, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  const st = w.st;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  switch (sheet) {
    case 4: // 火把 frameX ±66(:1207-1213)
      flipCell(w, x, y, 66);
      return true;
    case 10: // 关门 → 开(随机方向+反向,原版 :1171-1188)
      ctx.doorOpen(x, y);
      return true;
    case 11:
      ctx.doorClose(x, y);
      return true;
    case 15: { // 马桶椅样式（Wiring.cs:1498-1509 与 tile 497 共用分支；frameY/40==1 或 20）
      if (Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 40) !== 1 && Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 40) !== 20) return true;
      fireworkToilet(w, ctx, x, y);
      return true;
    }
    case 33: case 174: case 49: case 372: // 蜡烛族 frameX ±18(:1472-1480)
      flipCell(w, x, y, 18);
      return true;

---

export class Clock {
  /** 昼夜边界（1:1 Main.time：昼 4:30AM-7:30PM=54000t、夜 32400t；0=午夜线性 24h） */
  static readonly DAWN = 4.5 / 24;   // 0.1875
  static readonly DUSK = 19.5 / 24;  // 0.8125
  timeOfDay: number = 8.25 / 24;   // 0-1，0=午夜 0.5=正午（起始 8:15AM = Main.time 13500）
  dayCount: number = 1;
  /** 月相 0-7（0=满月 4=新月；每黎明 +1 mod 8，Main.cs:64877）。对话月相池/血月门用 */
  moonPhase: number = 0;
  /** 血月旗标（dusk roll / dawn 清除，Main.cs:64817-64831） */
  bloodMoon: boolean = false;
  /** 日食旗标（Main.eclipse，运行时态不进存档）：黎明 roll（Main.cs:64898，
   *  hardMode && downedMechBossAny && 1/20）/ 日耀碑牌召唤（Player.cs:43308，白天），
   *  夜幕清除（Main.cs:64721）——全天持续，只影响白天刷怪段 */
  eclipse: boolean = false;
  /** 圣诞季（现实日期 12/15-31，checkXMas Main.cs:13054） */
  xMas: boolean = false;
  /** 万圣季（现实日期 10/20-11/10，checkHalloween Main.cs:13093） */
  halloween: boolean = false;
  readonly dayLengthMs: number;
  constructor(dayLengthMs: number) { this.dayLengthMs = dayLengthMs; }
  tick(dtMs: number) {
    // 原版 Main.time 恒速 tick（1t/帧）：dayLengthMs=全周期 24min 时，0.625/0.375 的
    // 昼夜窗口自然得出 15min/9min——★勿再按段调速（曾除反导致昼夜缩水，探针抓回）
    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;
    while (this.timeOfDay >= 1) { this.timeOfDay -= 1; this.dayCount++; }
  }
  get isDay(): boolean { return this.timeOfDay > Clock.DAWN && this.timeOfDay < Clock.DUSK; }
  get dayFactor(): number {
    // 昼夜平滑系数：白天 1，夜晚 0.32（月光下地表仍可见，火把不再像贴在黑幕上），晨昏过渡
    const t = this.timeOfDay;
    if (t > Clock.DAWN + 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK - 0.04) return 1;
    if (t >= Clock.DUSK - 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK + 0.04) return 1 - (t - (Clock.DUSK - 0.04)) / 0.08 * 0.68;
    if (t >= Clock.DUSK + 0.04 || t < Clock.DAWN - 0.04) return 0.32;
    return 0.32 + (t - (Clock.DAWN - 0.04)) / 0.08 * 0.68;
  }

```

# 第三路审计报告：光照算法内核 + 天空/环境光 + 玩家手持光

原版标杆：`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/`（Terraria.Graphics.Light/*、Terraria/Lighting.cs、Terraria/Main.cs、Terraria/Player.cs）。本仓：`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/lighting/*` + `core/Game.ts` + `render/Renderer.ts`。

**总评**：Blur 传播内核（4 向扫描×2 pass、0.0185 死阈、衰减系数表、水衰减 13 式全表）确实做到了数值级 1:1；但发现 **4 个高价值语义偏差**：① 蜡烛/火把闪烁集合过宽（火把种子 1.0→1.16~1.32，照明半径 +3 格）；② 发光 tile 门控把致动块排除（原版 `active()` 含致动块）；③ `skySeed` 的昼夜窗口 (0.25,0.75) 与自家 Clock (0.1875,0.8125) 不一致 → 18:00–19:30 天空光差 4.4 倍；④ 月相地板表整体倒置。环境光链（血月、微光液体、云层/群系调制）缺失项也较集中。

| 1.2 | 同上：源格分支与死阈/写入是 **`if / else if`** 结构——本格是源格（cell>zero）时**不再做死阈判定** | `LightMap.ts:80-95`：拆成两个独立 `if`，源格复活后**仍执行** `zr<0.0185→dr=true` | **偏差（轻微）**：亮度 <1.85% 的源格会被立刻判死（原版可多传 1 格）。现有播种值均 ≥0.05，实际不可见，但结构不等价 |

| 3.4 | 地狱彩玻璃墙染色（:3273-3325）同天空表 | 同 2.11 的 TINT 表（含地狱分支未单独实现，共用天空段） | 偏差（同 2.11：绝对色而非 tileColor 乘区；此处原版基数是 num4 而非 tileColor，本仓 TINT 直接当绝对值，夜间/脉动相位均不跟随） |

## 5. 消费链公式（Lighting.GetColor → 画面）

| # | 原版 | 本仓 | 判定 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 5.1 | `Lighting.cs:12/88-91` `DEFAULT_GLOBAL_BRIGHTNESS=1.2`，`LightTiles` 每帧 `UpdateGlobalBrightness`（blind 时 1.0） | `lightTables.ts:48` `GLOBAL_BRIGHTNESS=1.2`（常量，无 blind 降档——blind 本就未实装） | 一致 |
| 5.2 | `Lighting.cs:217-237` `GetColor(int,int)`：`(int)(c×1.2×255)` **截断** + 钳 255；TileDrawing.cs:879 tile pass 用的正是这个重载 | `LightingEngine.ts:148-153` Present 期 `min(255, round(c×1.2×255))` 导出 Uint8 | 一致（语义）/ 偏差（微）：`Math.round` vs C# `(int)` 截断，每通道差 ≤1/255 |
| 5.3 | 引擎输出保持 **float Vector3**，逐绘制点现算 `GetColor`；`GetColor9Slice`（:251-273）用于坡面平滑 | Present 期一次性量化为 Uint8（lightAt 返回 0-255） | 偏差（微）：8bit 量化提前到 Present；原版也是最终 8bit（`new Color(Vector3)`），可见差异仅在亚阈值灰阶带 |
| 5.4 | tile pass **逐格平涂**（每 16px 块一个 GetColor），坡面/液体用 9-slice | `Renderer.ts:4658-4729` compositeLight：2× 超采样 + 4-tap 双线性 → 全屏 `multiply` | **偏差（结构性）**：光照被插值平滑（火把光斑比原版柔），且**全屏乘**会连带压暗 composite 之前已画的内容——`sky.draw` 在 :953、compositeLight 在 :1175 → **天空/背景层也被光照图相乘**；原版只乘 tile/wall/entity 精灵色，天空不吃光图。夜间天空种子≈0.11 → 本仓夜空≈原版亮度的 1/9，星月一并压黑 |
| 5.5 | `GetColor(x,y,oldColor)`（:203-208）= `light×oldColor` **不乘 1.2**（实体/物品用） | 所有消费统一用已含 ×1.2 的 Uint8（`mul(c,l)=c*l/255`，Renderer.ts:4617 等） | 偏差（微）：实体侧被多乘 1.2；因 Present 已全局烘焙，无法按消费方区分（原版 tile 乘 1.2、sprite 乘原始值） |
| 5.6 | 区域外 `GetColor=Zero`（LightingEngine.cs:61-70） | `lightAt` 区域外 `[0,0,0]`（LightingEngine.ts:157-161） | 一致（血条 alpha 有 `aQ<=0→0.15` 兜底，Renderer.ts:1624，为自定防全黑） |
| 5.7 | `Main.renderCount`/小地图导出（LightingEngine.cs:77-100, 238-265）、SceneMetrics 态 | `LightingEngine.ts:116-118` 显式留空槽保 4 tick 节奏 | 一致（有意省略，注释声明） |
